]
research = []
source = "arXiv"
summary = "Nereus 通过成本感知的在线 TP/PP 并行调整与分布式状态复用，在资源和负载动态变化时优化大模型强化学习后训练的执行效率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34645"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "Nereus 是面向大模型强化学习后训练的成本感知运行时，可随资源可用性、序列长度、显存压力和阶段瓶颈变化动态调整执行计划。其低开销控制器选择显存可行的全局计划，并利用基于当前作业校准的成本模型判断性能收益是否足以覆盖切换开销。系统以 Elastic Model Unit 表示各模型阶段副本的分布式状态，再通过全局转换图协调状态变换及跨模型、跨阶段的 GPU 迁移。实验中，在线 TP/PP 调整将平均单步延迟降低 27.7%，六次转换仅占千步运行总时间的 0.079%；8B PPO 吞吐量较 OpenRLHF 提升 2.14～7.27 倍，较 Verl 提升 1.10～1.47 倍。"
purpose = "- 为动态 GPU 集群上的强化学习后训练提供低开销、自适应的并行策略，避免固定 TP/PP 布局随负载变化而失效。\n- 展示如何将迁移成本纳入在线调度决策，防止频繁重配置带来的收益倒挂。\n- Elastic Model Unit 与全局转换图可为分布式训练状态复用、GPU 迁移编排和弹性运行时设计提供参考。\n- 结果表明，大规模后训练系统不仅需要数据并行扩缩容，还应联合优化张量并行与流水线并行布局。"
notes = "标题：Nereus: Adaptive Parallelism for LLM Post-Training\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34645\n摘要：Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training for large language models (LLMs) coordinates multiple models across generation, inference, and training on GPU clusters. Several factors may change during a run, including resource availability, sequence length, memory pressure, and stage bottlenecks. As a consequence, an execution plan that was initially suitable can then become slow or even infeasible over time. However, adapting a job whose models share GPUs entails significant challenges: deciding whether a new plan is worth the transition cost, reusing the job's distributed state, and coordinating GPU transfers across models and stages. Nereus targets these challenges as a cost-aware runtime that adapts RL post-training jobs into efficient execution plans. Its low-overhead controller selects a"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34636"
code = ""
dataset = ""

content = "MechReasoner 将机械式定性模拟器与推理基准结合，用于检验模型回答是否真正符合结构、因果与状态转移约束。基准包含 1,120 个确定性生成的样本，覆盖 18 类机械机制和六个任务族，并考察歧义保留、量化主张、情节图转移证据、修复及轨迹支持判断。每种机制均经过结构与拓扑转换检查，并使用定量模拟进行行为验证。实验中，GPT-5.5 的准确率从最低复杂度 B1 的 76.1% 降至最高复杂度 B4 的 38.0%，且控制提示及答案长度后负相关仍然存在。"
purpose = "- 为机械推理与定性物理推理提供结构化、可复现且可审计的模型评测方案。\n- 通过模拟器许可的状态、约束和推导轨迹，区分语言流畅性与真实的机制遵循能力。\n- 可用于研究模型在复杂度提升时的推理失效模式，并支持基于证据轨迹的可靠性诊断。\n- 展示了以形式化模拟器确定性生成高质量科学推理基准的可行路径。"
notes = "标题：MechReasoner: A Simulator and Benchmark for Mechanistic Reasoning in Qualitative Physics\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34636\n摘要：This work introduces MechReasoner, a mechanistic qualitative simulator grounded in confluence-based qualitative physics, together with a benchmark for mechanistic inference. Current large language models (LLMs) generate fluent mechanistic descriptions that do not reliably follow from underlying structural and causal constraints. The benchmark tests whether answers preserve simulator-licensed ambiguity, quantified claims, episode-graph transition evidence, repairs, and trace-support judgments. Its 1,120 items are generated deterministically from admissible interpretation sets, component states, scenario restrictions, confluence constraints, and derivation steps across 18 catalog mechanisms and six task families. Each mechanism undergoes converter checks of structure and topology and behavio"

title = "GenMem：面向自进化脚手架的生成式符号记忆"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34633"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "GenMem将智能体记忆管理重新表述为生成式符号寻址，以应对可复用经验稀疏、层次化且高度冗余的问题。其核心 Symbolic Identifier（SID）是多级离散词元元组，使用不足一百个离散符号对百万级稀疏记忆空间进行因子化寻址。记忆智能体生成稳定的 SID，而 MemEvolver 可在固定地址下重写记忆载荷，从而兼顾持续演化与寻址接口不变性。系统在多智能体脚手架中结合 MemRetriever 与 MemEvolver，并通过 GRPO、稠密过程奖励和结果奖励进行双通道优化，覆盖 ALFWorld、WebShop、多跳问答、医疗推理和深度研究等任务。"
purpose = "- 为长期运行智能体提供地址稳定、内容可持续修订的记忆架构。\n- 将大规模记忆检索转化为紧凑符号空间中的生成问题，降低稀疏冗余经验的管理难度。\n- 展示如何利用过程奖励与结果奖励缓解记忆生命周期中监督稀疏、反馈延迟的问题。\n- 可作为自进化智能体脚手架中经验保留、技能复用和记忆更新模块的设计参考。"
notes = "标题：GenMem: Generative Symbolic Memory for Self-Evolving Harness\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34633\n摘要：Long-term memory supports the self-evolution of LLM agents by retaining experience and skills across tasks and enabling their retrieval, reuse, and revision in subsequent long-horizon decision-making. Yet existing memory management approaches remain limited to discriminative retrieval and to address the sparse, hierarchical, and highly redundant structure of reusable experience: only a small, task-dependent subset of trajectories and memories warrants retention, retrieval, or revision. Learning these operations is further complicated by sparse, delayed, and indirect task-level feedback, with weak supervision across the memory lifecycle. Moreover, continual memory evolution introduces an architectural tension as addressing invariance: stored experience is perpetually revised, yet the addres"


[[items]]
id = "pmopd"
title = "PMOPD：基于参数更新子空间保护的多教师在策略蒸馏"

source = "arXiv"
summary = "PMOPD通过任务排序、循环训练和参数更新子空间投影抑制多教师在策略蒸馏中的跨任务干扰，实现代码、推理与数学能力的均衡提升。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34605"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "多教师在策略蒸馏需要将多种专门能力整合进共享参数，但容易出现提升一个领域却损害其他领域的“能力跷跷板”现象。研究发现，不同任务产生的参数更新会迅速集中到各自的低维子空间，为识别和控制跨任务干扰提供了几何依据。PMOPD利用各任务的累积参数位移构建子空间记忆，并投影梯度与优化器更新，以移除干扰受保护任务方向的分量。该方法还引入轻量级冲突探针指导任务排序，并通过循环策略兼顾子空间估计和任务及时重访；在Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B上的代码、推理、数学任务平均分分别提高2.54和2.09分。"
purpose = "- 为多教师蒸馏中的能力冲突提供可解释的参数更新几何分析框架。\n- 可通过子空间保护减少灾难性干扰，在共享模型中更均衡地融合多领域能力。\n- 任务冲突探针、排序和循环机制可用于优化多任务后训练的调度策略。\n- 表明几何感知优化能够作为可迁移组件应用于不同基础模型的能力整合。"
notes = "标题：PMOPD: Task Ordering, Cycling, and Parameter-Update Subspace Protection in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34605\n摘要：Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) has emerged as a popular post-training paradigm for integrating specialized capabilities in frontier language models. Existing OPD research has primarily focused on optimizing single-task distillation through objective design, distillation scope, and teacher signal construction, whereas MOPD must aggregate multiple capabilities in shared parameters and address the resulting capability seesaw, in which improving one domain suppresses capabilities acquired from another. Inspired by the distinctive update geometry of OPD, we find that parameter updates from different tasks rapidly concentrate in their respective low-dimensional subspaces during MOPD, providing a direct geometric basis for identifying and controlling cross-task interference. We there"


[[items]]
id = "item-157"
title = "修复后的智能体经验能否迁移至相关任务"
subtopic = "经验学习"
topics = [
    "经验复用",
    "能力迁移",

]
research = [
    "MemoryEvolve",
    "EvalEvolve",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究通过3300次运行比较失败经历修复前后的跨任务迁移效果，发现修复经验的价值与复用经验的价值并不等同，评估记忆更新需同时引入旧版本和独立执行基线。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34603"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究在100组 ThinkingBox 任务对和100组 APEX 任务对上开展3300次运行，将同一失败源任务修复前后的经验迁移到固定目标任务，并与独立执行比较。ThinkingBox 中 Full、Skill 和 Hybrid 修复分别带来44、29和32个百分点的增益，修复后表现也分别比独立执行高25、22和18个百分点，但任务族层面的统计证据较弱。Full 相对 Skill 多出的15个百分点修复差距中，有12个百分点源于修复前表现更差，而非修复后的记忆质量更高；其46次向上转变中也有22次只是恢复既有基线成功。APEX 的两种设置均未证明稳定的总体修复收益，且更短的经验交接虽然减少输入量，却会增加调用次数。结果表明，判断一次修复是否真正更新了可复用记忆，必须同时比较修复前版本和从零开始执行的参照结果。"
purpose = "- 为智能体长期记忆和经验库更新提供更严格的评估设计，避免把基线恢复误判为新能力获得。\n- 提醒研究者分别测量“修复经历是否改善”与“改善后的经历是否值得复用”，两者不能由单一成功率差值替代。\n- 可用于设计经验迁移实验的双参照框架：同时保留修复前历史和独立执行基线。\n- 表明工作流级经验中可复用的约束可能促进迁移，而源任务特有的局部选择和惯例冲突可能削弱迁移效果。"
notes = "标题：After the Fix: How Corrected Agent Histories Transfer to Related Tasks\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34603\n摘要：Does repairing an episode make its experience a better memory for the next task? We transfer the same failed source before and after accepted repair to a fixed target, alongside independent execution. Our 3,300 runs cover 100 ThinkingBox pairs and the same 100 APEX pairs with and without source-state inheritance, under eleven conditions. ThinkingBox's Full/Skill/Hybrid correction gains are 44/29/32 percentage points, with corrected performance 25/22/18 points above independence; inference weakens at the task-family level. Yet 12 of Full's 15-point larger correction gap over Skill come from worse uncorrected performance, not better corrected memory. Moreover, 22 of Full's 46 upward transitions restore observed baseline success. Neither APEX regime establishes comparable aggregate correction"

summary = "AgentWare 提供覆盖环境置备、分布式部署、可观测性与自动评估的 AgenticOps 框架，以降低异构边云基础设施上智能体应用的运维和实验成本。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34586"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "AgentWare 面向异构 Edge-to-Cloud 基础设施，自动准备执行环境，并将用户定义的智能体实现转换为分布式应用。框架支持跨边缘与云端部署智能体组件，统一收集执行轨迹、基础设施遥测数据和评估指标。它通过 LLM-as-a-Judge 工作流执行语义评估，并生成涵盖正确性、性能、资源利用率及能耗的可复现报告。作者以真实边云环境中的分布式图书助手为案例，表明该框架能够显著减少部署、插桩和分析所需的人工工作。"
purpose = "- 为分布式智能体应用提供从开发实现到部署、监控和评估的一体化工程流水线。\n- 支持在不同硬件、模型和部署配置之间开展可复现的系统实验与性能比较。\n- 将执行轨迹、资源消耗、能耗和任务正确性统一纳入评估，有助于优化边云组件放置与模型选型。\n- AgenticOps 思路可用于建设面向生产环境的智能体持续交付、可观测性和验收体系。"
notes = "标题：AgentWare: Automating the Lifecycle of Agentic Applications across the Edge-to-Cloud Continuum\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34586\n摘要：Deploying LLM-enabled agentic applications across the Edge-to-Cloud continuum remains challenging due to hardware heterogeneity, deployment complexity, limited observability, and the lack of systematic evaluation methods. Existing solutions address agent development, observability, or benchmarking separately, offering limited support for the full lifecycle of distributed agentic applications. This paper presents AgentWare, an AgenticOps framework that automates the provisioning, deployment, observability, and evaluation of agentic applications across Edge-to-Cloud infrastructures. AgentWare introduces an end-to-end lifecycle pipeline that automatically prepares heterogeneous execution environments, transforms user-defined agent implementations into distributed applications, deploys agent c"


[[items]]
id = "nudgeability"
title = "Nudgeability：推理模型盲从置信信号，却无法追踪自身能力"
subtopic = "置信度评估"
topics = [
    "模型评估",
    "不确定性估计",
    "工具调用",
    "人工智能可靠性",
    "反事实推理",
]
research = [
    "EvalEvolve",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究提出 Nudgeability 评测，发现推理模型的工具委派决策会显著受置信或怀疑措辞影响，但这种响应与模型真实解题能力仅有很弱的对应关系。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34572"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究在相同推理轨迹的固定位置插入表达“自信”或“怀疑”的第一人称句子，再观察模型选择直接回答还是调用工具，以测量反思信号对委派行为的因果影响。作者将这种行为响应称为 Nudgeability，并以敏感性和针对性两个维度衡量：前者描述信号改变委派率的幅度，后者考察变化是否符合模型的真实能力。对 Qwen、Gemma、GLM 三个家族的九个中小型开放权重推理模型进行测试后，置信到怀疑条件下的委派率中位变化达到 20.6 个百分点，大型服务模型则达到 53～70 个百分点。然而，被诱导改变的决策中仅有中位数 42% 得到正确针对，相比随机选择基线只提升约 2 个百分点，说明模型会跟随置信语言，却不能可靠判断自身是否胜任。"
purpose = "- 为工具增强推理模型提供无需后训练的自我反思与委派行为评测方法。\n- 提醒开发者不要将模型对置信措辞的敏感反应误认为可靠的元认知或能力校准。\n- 可将敏感性与针对性分开优化，避免模型只是增加工具调用，却没有把工具预算用于真正困难的问题。\n- 置信语言构成强控制面，也可能被提示注入或错误自我报告操纵，因此部署时应结合外部验证器、能力预测器或任务级校准机制。"
notes = "标题：Nudgeability: Reasoning Models Follow Confidence Signals Without Tracking Their Own Competence\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34572\n摘要：Reasoning language models that can call tools must decide during inference whether to answer unaided or delegate. Any self-reflection mechanism for this must answer three questions: where the reflective signal comes from (verbal reports, output distributions, hidden states, a separate predictor), how it is presented to the model (numerical prediction, confidence token, prompt injection), and whether it changes the model's subsequent action. We isolate the third question. At a fixed point in otherwise identical reasoning trajectories, we insert a single first-person sentence expressing either confidence or doubt; the model then continues reasoning and chooses whether to answer directly or call a tool. Comparing these counterfactual continuations measures the causal effect of the reflective "


[[items]]
id = "flowstate-llm"
title = "FlowState：以执行状态作为长程 LLM 智能体记忆"
subtopic = "长期记忆"
topics = [
    "智能体记忆",
    "长程任务",
    "状态表征",
    "上下文压缩",
    "智能体",
]
research = [
    "MemoryEvolve",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "FlowState 通过持久化语义化执行状态并按需访问历史证据，在降低上下文成本的同时提升长程智能体任务的成功率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34565"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "FlowState 将执行状态视为可跨请求保留和重访的记忆，以统一当前决策与历史信息复用。系统保存带有语义类型的状态节点、节点关系及原始工具观测引用，将持久化存储与按需访问解耦。增量状态更新（ISU）根据新输入和反馈维护当前状态，渐进式状态访问（PSA）则在推理过程中按需揭示历史状态及支撑证据。相较使用相同 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 的全上下文基线，FlowState 在 MemoryArena 和 τ³-Bench 上分别提升 4.55 和 13.95 个百分点，同时将总词元消耗降低 43.2% 和 40.6%。"
purpose = "- 为长程智能体提供一种介于完整历史保留与有损摘要之间的结构化记忆方案。\n- 通过保存原始工具观测引用，使智能体能够在后续信息到来时重新审视历史决策并追溯证据。\n- ISU 与 PSA 的组合可用于降低长任务中的上下文长度、推理成本和信息遗漏风险。\n- 该方法提示智能体记忆系统应将“持久保留什么”与“何时读取什么”分开设计。"
notes = "标题：FlowState: Execution State as Memory for Long-Horizon LLM Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34565\n摘要：Long-horizon tasks require LLM agents to continually draw on information from earlier interactions. However, retaining the full history increases context costs, while compressing it risks losing details needed later, and the relevance of historical information often becomes apparent as the task progresses. To address these challenges, we propose FlowState, which treats execution state as memory that can be retained and revisited across requests, unifying current decision-making with the reuse of historical information. FlowState preserves semantically typed state nodes, their relations, and references to raw tool observations, separating persistent retention from on-demand access. Within a single execution loop, Incremental State Update (ISU) maintains the current state based on new inputs"


[[items]]
id = "realvr"
title = "ReaLVR：以视觉证据约束潜空间视觉推理"
subtopic = "视觉推理"
topics = [
    "潜空间推理",
    "视觉推理",
    "多模态",
    "信用分配",
    "强化学习",
]
research = []
source = "arXiv"
summary = "ReaLVR通过在模型自由运行的潜在轨迹中引入视觉证据监督，缓解潜在词元对关键图像变化不敏感的证据信用分配缺口，并将潜空间视觉推理扩展至235B模型。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34563"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "作者发现，依赖最终答案奖励进行GRPO训练时，潜在词元对足以改变正确答案的图像扰动响应较弱，形成“潜在证据—信用缺口”。ReaLVR对比正确答案与模型生成的错误答案，以判断潜在轨迹中需要加强监督的位置，同时对比相关与错配的视觉证据，明确应保留的信息。该方法在三个模型家族上稳定优于所评测的潜空间视觉推理基线，并在Qwen2.5-VL-7B的五任务平均成绩上达到63.7%。实验还首次展示了潜空间视觉推理可扩展至235B前沿模型，并带来更强的问题敏感性、视觉区域对齐和关键潜在词元依赖。"
purpose = "- 表明仅使用最终答案奖励不足以监督不可观测的潜在推理过程，需要显式解决视觉证据保留与跨潜在词元的信用分配。\n- 提供一种无需将中间推理完全语言化、直接监督模型自由运行潜在轨迹的方法。\n- 为分析潜在词元是否真正依赖视觉证据提供图像扰动、区域对齐和固定上下文依赖等诊断思路。\n- 证明潜空间视觉推理训练能够扩展到235B规模，为降低显式思维链开销的多模态推理系统提供参考。"
notes = "标题：Rethinking Latent Visual Reasoning: Grounding Latent Reasoning in Visual Evidence\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34563\n摘要：Latent visual reasoning (LVR) enables multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to perform intermediate computation in continuous latent tokens rather than expressing every reasoning step in words. However, unlike textual CoT, latent reasoning is not directly observable, making it difficult to supervise what latent tokens learn. In this work, we first conduct a thorough analysis of latent-token behavior and identify a latent evidence-credit gap: latent tokens respond only weakly to image perturbations that alter the correct answer. We hypothesize that this issue stems from the lack of explicit supervision during GRPO training. These findings suggest that a final-answer reward provides too little guidance on what visual evidence to preserve or how credit should be assigned across latent toke"


[[items]]
id = "memo-hamming"
title = "单层 MeMo 是随机化 Hamming 核分类器"
subtopic = "机制发现"
topics = [
    "人工智能",
    "机器学习",
    "人工智能可解释性",
    "模型开发",
    "机制发现",
]
research = []
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究从理论上将单层 MeMo 的理想检索规则刻画为基于位置 Hamming 核的多分类器，并分析随机编码引入的误差及其性能权衡。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34562"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究证明，单层 MeMo 的理想检索规则等价于一个基于位置 Hamming 核的多分类器。MeMo 使用高斯随机码表示序列特征和输出标签，因此实际得分可视为对理想分类器的双重随机化草图。作者在输入与输出码本相互独立的条件下，分别界定上下文草图和输出解码产生的误差，并给出恢复理想预测的基于间隔的保证。受控模拟验证了理论趋势；在受限的 WikiText-2 下一词元预测任务中，MeMo 展现出准确率、内存与吞吐量之间的实用权衡，尤其适合利用 GPU 并行矩阵运算。"
purpose = "- 为理解 MeMo 的关联记忆机制提供核方法视角，将其语言建模行为还原为可分析的分类过程。\n- 误差界可用于指导随机码维度、上下文长度和数据规模等参数选择。\n- 基于间隔的恢复条件为评估随机压缩是否保持理想预测提供理论依据。\n- 结果表明，单层 MeMo 可作为重视内存占用与 GPU 吞吐量场景中的轻量级下一词元预测方案。"
notes = "标题：Single-Layer MeMo as a Randomized Hamming-Kernel Classifier\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34562\n摘要：MeMo (Zanzotto et al., 2025) is a recent language-model architecture that stores associations between token contexts and next tokens in a correlation matrix memory. In this work, we study its single-layer form and show that its ideal retrieval rule is a multiclass classifier based on the positional Hamming kernel. The MeMo architecture represents both the sequence features and the output labels with Gaussian random codes. Its score is therefore a doubly randomized sketch of the ideal classifier. Under independent input and output codebooks, we bound the errors introduced by context sketching and output decoding, characterize their dependence on model and data parameters, and give a margin-based guarantee for recovering the ideal prediction. Controlled simulations support the trends predict"


[[items]]
id = "skillrubric"
title = "SkillRubric：多模态智能体的技能指导与评估规则协同演化"
subtopic = "技能演化"
topics = [

    "技能演化",

]
research = []
source = "arXiv"
summary = "SkillRubric 将面向智能体的技能指导与面向评估器的规则对齐，并通过交替协同演化提供细粒度过程奖励，提升多模态智能体的长程规划与工具使用能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34557"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "SkillRubric 将每项可复用技能表示为相互对齐的两部分：面向执行者的操作指导，以及面向评估器的评价规则。多模态验证器依据截图和工具输出检查技能目标，并将完成奖励与进度奖励归因到对应的执行轮次，从而缓解稀疏结果奖励对中间决策监督不足的问题。该方法采用交替协同演化机制：在冻结策略时通过成对轨迹验证指导修订，并在固定指导时离线更新评估规则。多个多模态智能体基准上的实验显示，该方法能稳定提升性能，演化后的技能也比旧版本更有助于规划和工具使用。"
purpose = "- 为长程多模态智能体提供与操作流程一致的细粒度过程监督。\n- 将技能知识同时用于行动指导和结果评估，降低人工构建大规模评价规则的成本。\n- 通过冻结变量后的交替验证减少指导与评估规则共同更新造成的反馈漂移。\n- 可用于改进工具调用、轨迹信用分配及基于技能库的智能体训练。"
notes = "标题：SkillRubric: Co-Evolving Actor Guidance and Evaluator Rubrics for Multimodal Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34557\n摘要：Recent work incorporates reusable skills distilled from past interactions into multimodal agent training, providing procedural guidance for long-horizon planning and tool use. However, policy optimization in these methods remains driven primarily by sparse outcome rewards, providing little supervision for intermediate decisions. Rubric-based rewards address this limitation through explicit intermediate criteria, but reliable rubrics are difficult to construct at scale and often disconnected from the procedure followed by the actor. We observe that a well-structured skill naturally specifies both how to act and what successful execution should achieve. Based on this insight, we introduce SkillRubric, which represents each skill through aligned actor-facing guidance and an evaluator-facing r"


[[items]]
id = "sgg-reflact"
title = "SGG-ReflAct：子目标引导反思提升长程推理可靠性"
subtopic = "长程任务执行"
topics = [
    "长程任务",
    "智能体",
    "任务规划",

]
research = []
source = "arXiv"
summary = "SGG-ReflAct将规划器生成的中间子目标纳入逐步反思，并通过结构化计划探索减少智能体在长程任务中的目标漂移与幻觉动作。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34548"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "SGG-ReflAct针对ReflAct仅围绕最终目标反思、忽略中间子目标的问题，使用单路径LLM规划器生成子目标并将其融入反思过程。扩展版本BeamSGG-ReflAct采用基于束搜索的LLM规划器，以探索多个结构化计划。在ALFWorld、ScienceWorld和Jericho上的实验表明，该方法几乎在所有设置中都优于ReflAct，其中使用Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct时，前两个环境的最高成功率分别提升14.9和8.0个百分点。分析显示，该方法能减少幻觉动作，并在具有严格程序顺序的任务上获得最大收益；但束搜索实验也说明，明确计划中的必要操作比单纯扩大计划搜索更重要。"
purpose = "- 为长程智能体提供一种易于集成的子目标引导反思骨干，降低执行过程中的目标漂移。\n- 表明中间子目标可作为校准内部信念和检测阶段性错误的结构化依据。\n- 提示计划质量比单纯增加搜索宽度更关键，应明确建模任务所需操作及其执行顺序。\n- 可用于改进具身环境、交互式游戏及其他程序化多步任务中的智能体可靠性。"
notes = "标题：SGG-ReflAct: Sub-Goal Guided ReflAct with Structured Planning for Reliable Long-Horizon Reasoning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34548\n摘要：Recent advances in reasoning backbones have empowered large language model (LLM)agentstotackle complex, multi-step tasks. However, as reasoning horizons grow, inconsistent internal beliefs induce intermediate errors that cause agents to drift from their goals. This limitation also persists in REFLACT, which reflects only on the end-goal at each step without explicitly considering intermediate sub goals. To address this problem, we propose SGG-ReflAct (Sub-Goal Guided Re flAct), a reasoning backbone that integrates sub-goals generated through a single path LLM planner into the reflection process. We further extend this framework to BeamSGG-ReflAct, which replaces the single-path planner with a beam search based LLM planner for structured plan exploration. We run experiments on ALF World, Sc"


[[items]]
id = "memdream"
title = "MemDream：面向自探测记忆演化的离线梦境框架"
subtopic = "长期记忆"
topics = [
    "智能体记忆",
    "自演化",
    "智能体",
    "强化学习",
    "长程任务",
]
research = [
    "MemoryEvolve",
]
source = "arXiv"
summary = "MemDream通过多智能体离线梦境周期主动探测、诊断并修复记忆图，在真实查询暴露故障前持续演化大模型智能体的长期记忆。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34545"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "MemDream针对现有智能体记忆系统只能在真实查询失败后被动修复的问题，引入周期性的离线“梦境”阶段。Dreamer、Analyst和Consolidator三个专用智能体协作生成探测、诊断记忆缺陷，并对记忆图执行修复。框架使用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）训练策略，以学习能够带来持久检索改进的修复操作，同时通过软衰减机制实现可逆遗忘。在LoCoMo上，MemDream将答案F1提升4.5个百分点；在MemoryAgentBench上，其总体得分比最强的反应式演化基线高9.1个百分点。"
purpose = "- 将记忆维护从故障后的被动修复转变为交互前的主动诊断，可降低长程任务中的真实失败成本。\n- 展示了多智能体分工与强化学习策略结合进行记忆图持续演化的可行路径。\n- 软衰减提供了可逆遗忘机制，可在清理低价值记忆的同时减少不可恢复的信息损失。\n- 可用于构建具备长期个性化、主动维护和持续改进能力的智能体记忆系统。"
notes = "标题：Remember Before You're Asked: MemDream for Self-Probing Memory Evolution\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34545\n摘要：Memory is essential for enabling LLM-based agents to maintain coherent, personalized behavior over long-horizon interactions. However, existing memory systems share a fundamental limitation: they never proactively test their own memory, repairing it only after real queries expose weaknesses. This reactive paradigm means every retrieval failure corresponds to a real interaction in which the cost has already been paid. We propose MemDream, a framework that enables self-probing memory evolution for LLM agents. Our framework periodically enters offline dream cycles where three specialized agents (Dreamer, Analyst, Consolidator) collaboratively probe, diagnose, and repair the memory graph before failures occur. A policy trained via Group Relative Policy Optimization learns which repair operatio"

id = "sciarp"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34537"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "SciARP 将 620 个科学问题转换为由 3—13 轮相互依赖任务组成的评测样本，并设计了覆盖问题理解、证据处理、推理和结论形成的 13 类扰动。基准在匹配设置下分别运行干净版本与扰动版本，生成成对的真实执行轨迹，同时评估任务成功率和过程可靠性。对四个模型家族的八个 LLM 进行实验后发现，智能体即使信息或推理已不可靠，仍可能继续推进任务。更强的干净任务性能不一定带来更高鲁棒性，扰动影响还可能潜伏数轮后才显现，并沿下游依赖持续传播。"
purpose = "- 为科学智能体提供面向多轮交互全过程的扰动鲁棒性评测框架。\n- 提醒开发者不要仅用最终任务成功率衡量可靠性，应同步审计证据处理、推理过程和错误传播轨迹。\n- 可用于定位不同扰动类型的失效模式，并研究早期检测、执行恢复和过程验证机制。\n- 表明基础能力提升不等于鲁棒性提升，模型选型与训练应单独纳入抗扰动指标。"
notes = "标题：The Marathon of Scientific Reasoning: Robustness of Scientific Agents to Perturbations in Multi-Turn Interactions\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34537\n摘要：Large language model (LLM)-based scientific agents are increasingly used for scientific problem solving, yet their robustness to imperfections arising during multi-turn interactions remains poorly understood. We introduce \\textsc{SciARP} (\\textbf{Sci}entific \\textbf{A}gent \\textbf{R}obustness to \\textbf{P}erturbations), a benchmark for evaluating scientific agents under scientifically plausible perturbations throughout multi-turn problem solving. \\textsc{SciARP} transforms 620 scientific problems into interdependent tasks of 3--13 turns and defines 13 perturbation types spanning problem understanding, evidence processing, reasoning, and conclusion formation. Clean and perturbed versions of each task are independently executed under matched settings, producing paired live trajectories for e"


[[items]]
id = "pairpref"

source = "arXiv"
summary = "PairPref 通过仅改变情境的成对样本评测模型能否正确判断记忆中的用户偏好何时适用，结果显示现有模型普遍存在不加区分地套用偏好的问题。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34526"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "- PairPref 包含 1,227 组配对样本，覆盖 45 种用户偏好和八类情境；每组仅改变情境，偏好、请求及四个候选回复均保持不变。\n- 基准设置选择与自由生成两条赛道：前者要求从固定回复中选出仅在适当情境下采用偏好的答案，后者要求模型自行决定是否采用偏好。\n- 八个受测模型的选择赛道分数 Δ 多为 51～65 分，但自由生成中，成对回复均符合各自情境的比例仅为 3.6%～18.3%。\n- 减少检索记忆、改变偏好呈现格式或采用更严格提示均未解决问题，模型仍倾向于在两种情境中一概套用用户偏好。"
purpose = "- 将记忆系统评测从“能否存储与检索偏好”扩展到“能否判断偏好的情境适用性”。\n- 可用于诊断个性化助手中的偏好过度应用问题，避免记忆检索正确但响应决策错误。\n- 提示记忆增强系统需要在检索之后增加情境匹配、适用性判断或偏好门控机制。\n- 成对最小情境变化的设计有助于隔离检索相似度影响，可作为记忆决策可靠性的针对性测试方法。"
notes = "标题：PairPref: When Should Memory Guide the Answer? A Benchmark for Contextual Preference Use\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34526\n摘要：Memory-augmented assistants use retrieved preferences to guide their responses. A small change in the situation can change whether a preference is appropriate while barely affecting its retrieval similarity. Memory benchmarks typically test whether systems store and retrieve preferences, with less attention to when those preferences should apply. We introduce PairPref, a benchmark of contextual preference use. Each pair changes only the situation, keeping the preference, request, and four candidate replies fixed. The preference remains valid in both situations. In the selection track, models must choose the reply that applies the preference only where appropriate. In the free-generation track, they must decide when to apply it without seeing candidate replies. Both tracks use the same 1,22"


[[items]]
id = "eopsa"
title = "EOPSA：高效在策略自蒸馏安全对齐"
subtopic = "在策略蒸馏"
topics = [
    "安全对齐",
    "模型蒸馏",
    "模型训练",
    "成本优化",
    "推理",
]
research = []
source = "arXiv"
summary = "EOPSA通过自适应 rollout 调度与选择性蒸馏，仅训练安全关键 token，在降低约50% rollout 计算量的同时改善安全合规性并保留推理能力。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34519"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文指出，传统在策略自蒸馏安全对齐存在长 rollout 下教师纠正能力衰退，以及特权提示引发的风格差异稀释安全梯度两项瓶颈。EOPSA提出自适应 Rollout 调度，利用 Teacher Rescue Rate（TRR）动态限制生成长度，使训练保持在教师监督可靠的区间。其选择性蒸馏机制过滤安全中性 token，仅对安全决策关键的状态转移进行梯度更新。在最高32B参数的推理模型上，该方法减少约50%的 rollout 计算量，仅反向传播约2%的 token，并在安全合规和推理能力保持方面优于全 token 蒸馏基线。"
purpose = "- 为在策略安全蒸馏提供更高效的训练方案，减少无效 rollout 与反向传播开销。\n- 表明安全对齐应优先识别监督可靠区间及安全关键 token，而非对完整响应进行均匀蒸馏。\n- TRR可作为动态控制生成长度和监测教师纠正有效性的训练指标。\n- 有助于缓解安全对齐对基础推理能力的损害，适用于大规模推理模型后训练。"
notes = "标题：EOPSA: Efficient On-Policy Self-Distilled Safety Alignment\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34519\n摘要：On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a promising paradigm for safety alignment, delivering dense, token-level supervision by distilling from a teacher conditioned on refusal-oriented privileged prompts. However, we reveal that this paradigm suffers from critical inefficiencies that degrade both training efficiency and general reasoning capabilities. Specifically, we diagnose two fundamental bottlenecks: (1) supervisory collapse over extended rollouts, where the teacher's corrective efficacy degrades precipitously as the student's generation prefix lengthens, injecting noisy gradients into late-stage tokens; and (2) gradient dilution from stylistic shifts, where the distillation objective is dominated by safety-irrelevant stylistic discrepancies induced by privileged prompting,"


[[items]]
id = "sead"
title = "SEAD：工具型智能体的状态化攻防框架"
subtopic = "状态安全"
topics = [
    "智能体安全",
    "状态追踪",
    "工具调用",
    "运行时干预",
    "执行验证",
]
research = []
source = "arXiv"
summary = "SEAD将工具型智能体攻防建模为部分可观测的状态控制问题，并通过DART攻击搜索与SAGE执行前防御显著降低可执行攻击成功率。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34518"
code = "https://github.com/EverywhereSafety/SEAD"
dataset = "https://github.com/EverywhereSafety/SEAD"
link = ""
content = "SEAD指出，工具调用会持续改变文件、权限和数据库记录等外部状态，因此表面正常的后续动作可能因隐藏状态而产生危害。攻击方法DART将恶意目标分解为局部合理的步骤，并利用真实工具执行反馈搜索有效攻击轨迹；防御方法SAGE则在执行前通过只读查询调查相关状态，再决定允许或阻止动作。作者还构建了包含受控初始状态、可回放工具环境和任务级可执行检查的数据集。在在线攻防评测中，SAGE将DART的可执行攻击成功率从48.0%降至4.0%，并表现出跨攻击方法和域外环境的泛化能力。"
purpose = "- 为工具型智能体安全提供以外部状态为核心的统一攻防建模视角。\n- 说明仅审查当前指令或动作不足以判断风险，防御系统需要追踪并主动调查执行环境状态。\n- 可借鉴SAGE的只读查询与执行前拦截机制，构建低副作用的智能体运行时安全层。\n- 可利用其可回放环境和可执行检查方法，提升智能体攻击评测的真实性与可验证性。"
notes = "标题：SEAD: A State-Based Perspective on Attack and Defense in Tool-Using Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34518\n摘要：Language-model agents increasingly use tools to act on external systems. Earlier actions can alter files, permissions, database records, or other state, making a later routine-looking action harmful. Yet the visible interaction may not reveal the underlying state needed to assess that action. We formulate attack and defense as partially observed state control in SEAD, deriving their design requirements from this shared execution process. Because attackers supply instructions while the target chooses concrete actions, DART decomposes harmful goals into locally plausible steps and uses feedback from actual tool execution to guide trajectory search. The defender must decide before execution with incomplete state evidence. SAGE can therefore investigate relevant state through read-only queries"

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34510"
code = "https://github.com/Starlien95/Awesome-TradingAI"
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究提出一个面向加密货币市场的统一交易评测基准，涵盖机器学习、强化学习、LLM 和交易智能体等代表性方法。评测分为历史回测、基于交易所的前瞻性模拟盘和真实资金实盘交易三个阶段，逐步提高时间与执行环境的真实性。该协议纳入延迟、滑点、流动性约束和市场冲击等交易摩擦，用于定位策略性能从何时开始恶化。作者还提供支持三阶段评测的开源系统，以及持续更新基准结果的公共平台。"
purpose = "- 衡量历史回测收益能否泛化至未知的未来市场，避免仅凭离线结果判断交易策略有效性。\n- 分阶段识别延迟、滑点、流动性和市场冲击对不同 AI 交易方法的影响。\n- 为机器学习、强化学习、LLM 与交易智能体提供统一且更贴近真实市场的比较框架。\n- 可作为金融智能体部署前的分层验收流程，从低成本回测逐步过渡到模拟盘和小额实盘。"
notes = "标题：Can AI Make Money in Crypto? Measuring the Gap from Backtests to Real Markets\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34510\n摘要：AI-based trading methods have rapidly evolved from machine learning and reinforcement learning to large language models (LLMs) and trading agents, yet their performance is still predominantly assessed through historical backtesting. Such evaluations provide limited evidence of whether a method can generalize to unseen future markets or whether its backtested performance can be sustained in realistic trading frictions (e.g., latency, slippage, liquidity constraints, and market impact). We present a unified benchmark that evaluates representative machine learning, reinforcement learning, LLM-based, and agent-based trading methods in cryptocurrency markets through three progressively more realistic stages: historical backtesting, prospective exchange-based paper trading, and real-money live t"

[[items]]
id = "harness-learning"

source = "arXiv"
summary = "该研究将智能体脚手架的修改建模为可执行程序上的元学习，并训练提议模型利用执行反馈在测试时持续优化新任务的求解流程。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35738"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "语言模型智能体由模型及其脚手架共同定义，后者负责组织模型调用、工具使用与信息流。论文提出 Harness Learning，由提议模型依据执行反馈修改求解模型的脚手架，以适配具体任务。该过程被表述为可执行程序上的元学习，其中脚手架修订对应梯度式适应中的权重更新。提议模型通过强化学习训练，以修订后脚手架的任务表现作为奖励；测试时则根据新任务上的连续执行反馈迭代改进脚手架。"
purpose = "- 为固定模型提供无需更新模型权重的测试时任务适应机制。\n- 说明智能体能力优化可从模型参数扩展到可执行脚手架及信息流设计。\n- 可用于自动改进工具调用、模型调用编排和任务求解流程。\n- 为脚手架演化与元学习、强化学习的结合提供统一建模思路。"
notes = "标题：Harness Learning Enables Generalizable Test-Time Adaptation\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35738\n摘要：A language-model agent is jointly defined by its model and its harness, the executable program that organizes model calls, tool use, and information flow. Because different tasks call for different ways of organizing these operations, the harness needs to be adapted using feedback from the task at hand. We introduce harness learning, which trains a proposer model to revise a solver's harness using execution feedback. We formulate this process as meta-learning over executable programs, with harness revisions playing the role of weight updates in gradient-based adaptation. We train the proposer with reinforcement learning, using the task performance of revised harnesses as the reward. At test time, the proposer uses feedback from successive executions on a new task to refine the harness, wit"


[[items]]
id = "phonecli"
title = "PhoneCLI：将移动应用界面编译为可调用命令"
subtopic = "工具调用"
topics = [
    "智能体工具",
    "图形用户界面智能体",
    "工具调用",
    "执行验证",
    "成本优化",
]
research = []
source = "arXiv"
summary = "PhoneCLI 从应用外部探索并编译 GUI 导航路径，使移动智能体能够通过经过执行前验证的确定性命令替代重复、昂贵且脆弱的视觉操作循环。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35671"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "PhoneCLI 面向依赖截图、VLM 推理和动作输出循环的移动 GUI 智能体，旨在降低其导航延迟、调用成本与执行脆弱性。系统离线从应用外部进行探索，无需应用内部 API、运行时插桩或额外模型训练，并将屏幕、交互元素及导航边整理为带语义标注的地图。每个已发现的目标屏幕会被编译为一条确定性命令，其本质是可重复执行并抵达该屏幕的动作序列。在线阶段，智能体选择相应命令，在执行前进行验证，随后以确定性方式完成导航。"
purpose = "- 将高频、静态且重复的 GUI 导航从逐步 VLM 决策转化为可复用命令，可显著减少视觉模型调用和交互延迟。\n- 为缺少公开 API 的移动应用提供一种黑盒式工具封装方案，有助于扩展移动智能体的可操作范围。\n- 语义界面地图与执行前验证机制可提升导航的可预测性，并为失败恢复、轨迹审计和命令缓存提供基础。\n- 该方法启示智能体系统将模型用于高层意图选择，而把稳定的底层操作交给经过验证的确定性执行器。"
notes = "标题：PhoneCLI: From App Interfaces to Callable Commands for Mobile Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35671\n摘要：Mobile GUI agents operate through a perception--action loop: at each step they screenshot the device, invoke a vision--language model (VLM), and emit an action. It is slow, costly, and brittle, yet most of what it does is navigation---and everyday navigation is static, ordered, and endlessly repeated. We present PhoneCLI, which compiles an app's GUI navigation into callable commands, without any app-internal API, runtime instrumentation, or model training. Offline, PhoneCLI explores a target app from the outside and distills its screens, interactive elements, and navigation edges into a semantically annotated map; each screen yields one deterministic command: a replay sequence that reaches it. Online, the agent selects a command, verifies it before execution, and then executes it determini"


[[items]]
id = "item-175"
title = "谁在谁的左边？相对位置推理中的空间证据追踪与角色绑定"
subtopic = "视觉推理"
topics = [
    "视觉推理",
    "人工智能可解释性",
    "推理",
    "多模态",
    "因果归因",
]
research = []
source = "arXiv"
summary = "研究通过激活修补与定向干预，揭示视觉语言模型进行相对位置推理时从位置证据、查询对象角色到答案状态的分阶段表征过程。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35486"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "研究指出，较高的样本级准确率可能掩盖模型在对象交换位置或查询角色反转时的空间推理不一致性。作者在三种视觉语言模型及其语言模型骨干上，对视觉和文本输入进行激活修补分析。结果显示，相对位置推理呈现分阶段演化：早期层编码来源侧空间信息，中间层形成查询对象表征，后期层则逐步转化为答案状态。进一步的定向干预验证了这些阶段之间的因果联系，表明位置证据会沿内部计算路径传递并与查询角色绑定。"
purpose = "- 为诊断视觉语言模型是否真正理解相对空间关系提供机制层面的分析方法。\n- 提醒评测设计不能只看总体准确率，还应加入位置交换、角色反转等一致性测试。\n- 激活修补和定向干预可用于定位空间证据传递失败或角色绑定错误发生的具体网络层。\n- 相关发现有助于提升视觉问答、具身导航和空间规划模型的可解释性与可靠性。"
notes = "标题：Who Is Left of Whom? Tracing Spatial Evidence and Role Binding in Relative-Position Reasoning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35486\n摘要：High instance-level accuracy can mask inconsistencies in spatial reasoning when objects exchange positions or their roles are reversed in the query. The internal representations supporting relative-position reasoning remain poorly understood. We investigate two complementary components of this process: tracking object locations in the input and representing their query roles. Across three VLMs with visual or textual inputs and their language-model backbones, activation patching reveals a staged progression from early-layer source representations through intermediate-layer query-object representations to late-layer answer states. Targeted interventions further establish causal links along this progression: manipulating source-side representations shifts location information at query-object "

paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35315"
code = ""

link = ""
content = "COEVOLVE 将协作式科学发现建模为多个并行原则演化分支之间的证据迁移，以缓解顺序探索覆盖有限、耗时较长的问题。系统使用协调核心管理并行分支，使各分支共享有价值的测量证据，同时保留彼此独立的原则后验。其关键机制包括基于信息价值的门控路由，以及经过上下文折扣的似然注入，从而控制证据传播及其影响强度。论文在六项科学发现任务上进行实验，但所给摘要未完整展示具体结果。"
purpose = "- 为自动化科学发现提供可并行扩展的假设与原则探索机制，提升有限时间内的探索广度。\n- 通过信息价值门控减少低收益证据传播，帮助控制多分支协作的通信与推理成本。\n- 分离共享测量证据与各分支原则后验，可降低分支过早趋同和多样性坍缩的风险。\n- 可用于设计具备证据共享、独立推断和动态协调能力的科研智能体系统。"
notes = "标题：Collaborative Principle Evolution via Evidence Transfer for Scientific Discovery\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35315\n摘要：Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents promise to automate scientific discovery, yet exploring the vast hypothesis space remains costly. Existing principle-evolution methods accelerate this loop, but operate sequentially, which caps exploration breadth and wastes wall-clock time on challenging problems. To address this, we formulate collaborative scientific discovery as evidence transfer between parallel principle-evolution branches. We present COEVOLVE, which realizes this transfer through a coordination core over parallel branches. By integrating value-of-information-gated routing and context-discounted likelihood injection, COEVOLVE enables branches to collaborate through shared measurements while keeping their principle posteriors separate. Across six scientific-discovery tasks under "

Across six retro"

Existing rubric-guided approaches, however, exploit such guidance only at a coarse granularity, either by using rubrics to supervise the prediction of relevant aspects for subsequent generation or by reducing aspect coverage to a single response-level reward for reinforcement learning. This leaves a gap between specifying what a personalized answer should contain and teaching the model how to generate it. To bridge this gap, we propose GRASP, a rubric-aware on-policy self-distillation framework for LLM personalization that turns user-specific rubric aspects into f"

content = "论文提出 Imprint Reader，旨在将模型训练产生的参数级变化转化为明确的自然语言描述。其 Semantic Mount-and-Read Tuning 方法将冻结的权重更新挂载到 Reader 上，并通过无需锚点的元查询读取其中蕴含的学习信息。训练过程加入无变化与随机扰动对照，以抑制模型依赖表面线索或生成缺乏依据的解释。该研究进一步将权重更新的语义读出连接到行为干预，为模型反思自身学习过程提供技术路径。"

notes = "标题：Imprint Reader: From Weight-Update Readout to Behavioral Intervention\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35261\n摘要：As language models take a growing role in AI development, a natural aspiration is for them to reflect on their own learning process, as humans do, and use that reflection to improve themselves. At the same time, these models have an advantage that human learners lack, since training leaves parameter-level traces that can, in principle, be inspected directly. However, current models cannot decode these traces into an explicit account of what they have learned. To this end, we introduce the \\textit{Imprint Reader}, a model trained with \\textit{Semantic Mount-and-Read Tuning} (SaRT) to describe frozen weight updates. SMaRT mounts each update onto the Reader and uses an anchor-free meta-query to elicit a natural-language description, while no-change and random-perturbation controls discourage "

code = ""

link = ""
content = "ASCT面向智能体强化学习中终局效用难以归因到中间决策的问题，将训练阶段的多步搜索转化为局部动作信用。在智能体实际访问的状态上，辅助搜索树从可恢复的相同前缀出发，评估其他合法动作及其后续结果。得到的动作价值表以冻结智能体的动作概率为中心化基准，并用于对智能体采样轨迹执行PPO更新；部署时则只需保留智能体本身。该框架可采用均匀搜索、UCT和成本感知AgentUCT，并在HotpotQA智能体检索增强生成任务上优于基于整条轨迹回报的PPO。"
purpose = "- 为长程智能体任务提供比终局回报更细粒度的动作级信用分配信号。\n- 利用同一可恢复前缀下的反事实分支比较，降低中间决策价值估计的歧义。\n- 将搜索限制在训练阶段，避免辅助树搜索增加线上部署成本。\n- 可结合成本感知搜索，在信用估计质量与训练计算开销之间进行权衡。"
notes = "标题：ASCT: Attentive Search over Counterfactual Trees for Credit Assignment in Agentic Reinforcement Learning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35215\n摘要：Terminal utility evaluates a complete agentic workflow, but learning requires credit for the decisions within it. We introduce Attentive Search over Counterfactual Trees (ASCT), a framework that turns training-time multi-step search into local action credit. At actor-visited states, an auxiliary tree evaluates alternative legal actions from the same recoverable prefix. Its action-value table is centered by the frozen actor's probabilities and supplies credit for PPO on actor-sampled trajectories. This protocol connects counterfactual evaluation to policy learning while deploying the actor alone. Uniform, UCT, and cost-aware AgentUCT instantiate the framework. On HotpotQA agentic retrieval-augmented generation, all three improve mean held-out utility over trajectory-return PPO and workflow-"

research = []
source = "arXiv"
summary = "VSDD 将离散扩散训练建模为领导者—跟随者博弈，学习具备语义感知能力的前向扰动过程，使模型能够更有效地重写并纠正已生成词元。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35166"
code = ""

content = "离散扩散模型可在生成过程中重新审视并纠正早期词元，但这种重写能力高度依赖前向扰动过程。掩码扩散通常在词元解除掩码后将其固定，而均匀扩散虽允许修改，却只采用均匀随机的词元替换。VSDD 不再使用固定扰动规则，而是基于去噪器的词元嵌入学习具有语义感知能力的马尔可夫扰动过程。该方法将训练构造成变分 Stackelberg 领导者—跟随者博弈，使扰动策略主动选择最有助于去噪器学习重写的替换。"
purpose = "- 为离散扩散模型提供可学习的前向扰动机制，减少固定掩码或均匀随机替换带来的训练局限。\n- 展示博弈论方法在联合优化数据扰动策略与去噪模型方面的应用价值。\n- 可启发面向文本修订、迭代解码和错误纠正的生成模型设计。"
notes = "标题：Learning to Re-Draft: A Variational Stackelberg Game for Discrete Diffusion\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35166\n摘要：Discrete diffusion models offer the ability to re-draft, revisiting and correcting earlier tokens throughout generation. This capability depends on the forward corruption process that defines what the denoiser learns to correct. Masked diffusion models fix tokens once they are unmasked, while uniform diffusion permits revisions but relies on uniformly random token substitutions. We instead learn which substitutions are most useful for training the denoiser to re-draft. We introduce Variational Stackelberg Discrete Diffusion (VSDD), a framework for learning a semantically aware corruption process. VSDD formulates training as a leader-follower game: the leader defines a Markovian corruption process parameterized by the denoiser's token embeddings, while the follower optimizes a variational d"


[[items]]
id = "conflens"
title = "ConfLens：通过过早答案承诺检测捷径推理"
subtopic = "置信度评估"
topics = [
    "人工智能可解释性",
    "不确定性估计",
    "模型评估",
    "捷径学习",
    "推理",

research = []
source = "arXiv"
summary = "ConfLens 通过追踪大模型推理过程中对最终答案的置信度演化，以“过早置信”模式识别依赖捷径并事后合理化的非忠实推理。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35074"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "论文指出，语言模型输出的思维链不一定忠实反映其内部决策过程，模型可能先利用捷径确定答案，再生成连贯解释进行事后合理化。作者提出 ConfLens，在整个推理生成过程中持续追踪模型对最终答案的置信度变化，而非仅检查文本轨迹或最终结果。在三类捷径推理设置中，捷径样本呈现出共同的“过早置信”现象，即模型在充分展开推理前便对最终答案表现出较高置信度。该信号为检测表面合理但实际依赖捷径的推理过程提供了新的行为层诊断视角。"
purpose = "- 可用于构建不依赖思维链表面文本的捷径推理检测器，提高推理过程审计的可靠性。\n- 将置信度随生成进程的动态变化纳入评估，有助于区分真实逐步推理与答案先行的事后合理化。\n- 可作为过程监督、模型评判和安全审计的辅助信号，用于发现最终答案正确但推理机制不可信的样本。\n- 启示评测设计应关注信念形成的时间结构，而不应只依据最终置信度或文本连贯性。"
notes = "标题：When Confidence Rises Too Early: Detecting Shortcut Reasoning via Premature Answer Commitment\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35074\n摘要：The reasoning trajectory of a Large Language Model (LLM) is often treated as a verbalized description of its internal reasoning. However, such trajectories can be unfaithful: a model may rely on shortcuts to reach an answer and then post-rationalize the decision with a seemingly coherent chain of thought. Detecting this shortcut reasoning is challenging because existing monitors and verifiers mainly inspect textual traces or final outcomes, rather than how the model's belief in its answer develops during generation. We introduce ConfLens, a framework that tracks the evolution of confidence in the final answer throughout reasoning. Across three shortcut reasoning settings, we observe a common pattern of premature confidence, where shortcut samples become highly confident in the final answer"

notes = "标题：TIDE: Teacher-Student Transition via Informative Distillation and Exploration for Agentic RL\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35058\n摘要：Effective multi-turn agents require interaction strategies that coordinate information gathering, actions, and feedback over long horizons. Recent methods augment RL with on-policy distillation (OPD) from a stronger teacher. However, a fixed mixture assumes that teacher guidance and reward optimization should retain a constant relative role throughout training and across interaction turns. This assumption can fail at two scales. Globally, as training progresses, maintaining strong distillation pressure can constrain the model from moving beyond the teacher's capabilities. Locally, teacher--student disagreement identifies where the student depar"

notes = "标题：The Right Lesson at the Right Step: Deriving Control Updates for Self-Evolving Agents\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34988\n摘要：Self-evolving agents improve future behavior by reusing past experience, typically as global prompts, memories, or reflections. Yet these mechanisms rarely control where experience takes effect. In long tool-use workflows, the same lesson may correct one decision but distract another, making experience reuse a problem of localized control rather than memory alone. We introduce EvoCUE (Evolution through Control Updates from Evidence), a framework for learning reusable control-program updates from completed agent executions. EvoCUE represents the agent as an explicit state-machine controller, whose nodes perform model or tool calls and whose edges define where control passes next. This makes the workflow editable at precise locations, so each learned update can specify what to add, where it "


[[items]]
id = "teach-to-learn-llm"
title = "Teach to Learn：提示退火驱动 LLM 推理自我改进"
subtopic = "策略优化"
topics = [
    "强化学习",

    "推理",
]
research = [

source = "arXiv"
summary = "该研究通过提示退火缓解 GRPO 中困难样本缺乏奖励对比及提示轨迹分布偏移的问题，使模型逐步学会在无提示条件下完成推理。"
paper = "https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34975"
code = ""
dataset = ""
link = ""
content = "GRPO 依赖同一问题多条 rollout 之间的可验证奖励差异，但困难问题可能产生全错轨迹，导致训练信号消失。已有方法利用答案证据构造辅助提示并重新求解失败问题，却将提示条件下生成的轨迹视作普通轨迹，而评测时通常没有这些提示。论文指出这会产生“提示奖励偏移”：恢复出的奖励对比可能让策略更新过度集中于提示轨迹，从而限制模型无提示推理能力的提升。为此，研究引入提示退火机制，逐步削弱训练阶段的提示依赖，将辅助求解获得的学习信号迁移到自主推理过程。"
purpose = "- 为 GRPO 在困难样本全部失败时恢复有效奖励对比和策略梯度。\n- 揭示辅助提示轨迹与无提示评测条件之间的分布偏移风险。\n- 提供从有提示学习逐步过渡到自主推理的训练思路，降低模型对外部解题线索的依赖。\n- 可用于改进推理模型的强化学习课程设计与困难样本利用效率。"
notes = "标题：Teach to Learn: Hint Annealing for Self-improving LLM Reasoning\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34975\n摘要：Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) improves language-model reasoning by comparing verified rewards among multiple solution rollouts for each query. However, difficult training queries can yield only incorrect rollouts, leaving GRPO with no reward contrast or learning signal. Prior hint-based methods construct auxiliary hints from solution evidence and use them to re-solve failed queries, recovering learning signal. Yet the resulting trajectories are typically treated as ordinary solution trajectories despite being generated under an assisted condition unavailable at evaluation. We discover hinted reward shift: recovered reward contrast can concentrate policy updates on hinted trajectories, limiting improvement without hints. This also creates a trade-off: increasing hinted trajector"

code = ""

content = "ProofLoom 面向研究级随机优化算法的自动形式化，可根据已发表算法、目标定理和原始证明，自主构建 Lean 中的算法模型及其支撑理论。系统以尚未解决的证明义务为反馈，持续生成或调整定义、接口、引理与证明计划。其签名契约记录模型修订所依赖的证据和待履行义务，以避免为了可证明性而悄然改变数学命题。独立 Judge 会拒绝缺乏依据的假设和被弱化的结论，Planner 则把论文中的论证展开为可验证的中间命题。"
purpose = "- 为复杂数学论文从自然语言证明到 Lean 形式化提供端到端自动化路径。\n- 展示了以证明义务作为智能体开发反馈信号，可推动模型、理论接口与证明计划协同演化。\n- 签名契约与独立评判机制可用于约束自动形式化过程中的命题漂移和隐含假设。\n- 该方法可扩展到随机优化之外、同样依赖大量领域支撑理论的研究级数学成果。"
notes = "标题：ProofLoom: Proof-Obligation-Driven Theory Construction for Autoformalizing Research-Level Stochastic Optimization\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34960\n摘要：Formalizing research-level stochastic optimization in Lean requires both an algorithm model and domain theory connecting foundational libraries to convergence proofs. Revising a model to restore provability can change the mathematical claim. We introduce ProofLoom, a fully automated LLM-agent system for Proof-Obligation-Driven Theory Construction. Given a published algorithm, target theorem, and source proof, ProofLoom autonomously constructs the Lean model and supporting theory. Open proof obligations drive the development of definitions, interfaces, lemmas, and proof plans. Signature contracts record evidence and obligations for model revisions; an independent Judge rejects unsupported assumptions and weakened conclusions. Planner expands the published argument into intermediate claims, "

notes = "标题：Proactive Dialogue Policy Optimization via Cognitive-State Transition\n来源：arXiv\n链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34948\n摘要：Proactive dialogue requires agents to continually adapt their policies to user feedback while progressing toward task objectives over multiple turns. However, many simulators do not explicitly model the evolution of user cognition, limiting the consistency and state dependence of feedback across turns. Moreover, representing each action only by a high-level strategy label overlooks the large utterance space and cannot distinguish alternative realizations of the same strategy. To this end, we jointly design a $\\textbf{Cog}$nitive User $\\textbf{Sim}$ulator $\\textbf{(Cog-Sim)}$ and $\\textbf{C}$ognitive-$\\textbf{S}$tate $\\textbf{T}$ransition--Driven $"

<!-- daily-summary:start -->

## 今日概览
- **递归自我改进从模型闭环走向脚手架与研发系统演化**：从 AI 深度参与完整研发闭环的 [Naive AI 开源 AI 研发模型 Naive-N0.5-Flash](#naive-ai-ai-naive-n0-5-flash)，到以结构化实验约束自主后训练的 [RSI-Master：以结构化实验引导自主模型改进](#rsi-master)，多项工作共同强调：持续突破依赖可达工具、验证器和任务分解空间的扩展，而非仅反复优化固定检查点。
- **长程智能体的核心竞争转向记忆、上下文与可恢复状态**：[连续上下文管理：逐轮压缩长程智能体记忆](#item-58)、[FlowState：以执行状态作为长程 LLM 智能体记忆](#flowstate-llm) 和 [Planarian：用状态点统一管理智能体状态](#planarian) 分别从压缩、语义状态与快照回滚切入；同时，隐私披露、错误记忆和跨智能体传播成为必须同步治理的新风险。
- **智能体评测快速迈向动态、过程化和真实部署驱动**：[TraceDance：从真实部署轨迹自动构建智能体行为基准](#tracedance) 将线上缺陷转成针对性测试，[WebPageBench：基于事件验证与可控 UI 变体的网页智能体评测](#webpagebench-ui) 用事件日志和界面变体检验稳健性，[AutoDataBench：评测智能体能否生成自我改进所需数据](#autodatabench) 则开始直接衡量系统生产下一轮训练数据的能力。
- **强化学习与在策略蒸馏集中攻关信用分配、教师冲突和训练效率**：[CRBC：长程智能体强化学习的跨轨迹贝尔曼闭包](#crbc) 与 [DCSD：解耦信用方向与幅度的自蒸馏方法](#dcsd) 改善局部信用估计，[领域归一化的多教师在策略蒸馏](#item-8) 和 [PMOPD：基于参数更新子空间保护的多教师在策略蒸馏](#pmopd) 则针对多教师能力迁移失衡与干扰提出互补方案。
- **多智能体扩展同时暴露协作结构与群体安全问题**：[Noam Brown谈多智能体扩展与递归自我改进](#noam-brown) 强调并行 Agent 带来的测试时扩展潜力，[SAGE：自适应专家组提升多智能体推理](#sage) 展示动态选择与稀疏路由收益；但 [共享载体 AI 病毒：跨智能体记忆跳跃攻击](#item-50) 和 [同质 LLM 辩论中的崩塌与纠正测量](#llm-3) 表明共享记忆与意见传播也会放大攻击和集体误判。
- **生产级智能体基础设施在降本、隔离与长时运行上同步推进**：[阿里云 Agent Sandbox：面向智能体的云上安全执行底座](#agent-sandbox) 提供 MicroVM 隔离和弹性并发，[OpenAI 升级 Codex Cloud，Agents API 支持 computer use](#openai-codex-cloud-agents-api-computer-u) 强化可复用云环境，而 [Fireworks AI 发布缓存感知路由器 FireRouter with Opus](#fireworks-ai-firerouter-with-opus) 与 [TokenCast：预测 LLM 智能体执行中的 Token 消耗](#tokencast-llm-token) 展示按任务和轨迹精细控制成本的趋势。

## 对当前研究的启发
- **Awesome-RSI**：[审计脚手架而非检查点：智能体编程递归自我改进的平稳性二分](#item-114) 给出了明确研究假设——固定编辑空间终将边际递减，RSI 评测应重点追踪工具、验证器和任务分解所定义的可达空间是否持续扩张。
- **DataEvolve**：[AutoDataBench：评测智能体能否生成自我改进所需数据](#autodatabench) 可作为数据飞轮的生产验收层，并可结合 [通过预测误差将内部表征转化为模型改进](#item-65) 的无标签数据价值估计来优化样本筛选。
- **EnvironmentEvolve**：[F4R：失败驱动的机器人持续自我改进框架](#f4r) 表明可将真实执行失败自动重建为针对性仿真环境，为“失败轨迹→环境合成→联合训练→现实复验”的环境进化闭环提供直接范式。
- **EvalEvolve**：[TraceDance：从真实部署轨迹自动构建智能体行为基准](#tracedance) 与 [SymbolicArena：符号回归基准蒸馏与动态评测基础设施](#symbolicarena) 分别提供从部署缺陷生成新题和压缩基准成本的方法，可组合成持续更新且可控成本的动态评测流水线。
- **EvolveLLM**：[仅靠自我回溯微调提升智能体，无需强化学习](#item-36) 说明自生成失败解释本身可成为持续学习信号，而 [EvoIn：融合演化与内化的智能体微调框架](#evoin) 进一步提供了将脚手架中验证过的能力内化进基础模型的路径。
- **EvolveLRM**：[潜空间推理涌现可泛化的循环搜索算法](#item-43) 与 [Teach to Learn：提示退火驱动 LLM 推理自我改进](#teach-to-learn-llm) 提示可联合研究“潜在循环计算＋逐步撤除外部提示”，以检验搜索算法能否被稳定内化并泛化到更深推理。
- **Groom**：[TokenCast：预测 LLM 智能体执行中的 Token 消耗](#tokencast-llm-token) 的分段成本表征和 [AgentPerfBench：智能体大模型推理性能评测套件](#agentperfbench) 的真实轨迹负载可用于建立规划、工具反馈、恢复等阶段的 token 归因基线，而非只比较总成本。
- **HarnessEvolve**：[Harness Learning：可泛化的测试时脚手架自适应](#harness-learning) 与 [ContextEvo：面向长程智能体脚手架的自进化上下文策略](#contextevo) 表明 harness 可被建模为基于执行反馈持续修改的程序，适合进一步研究改动归因、跨任务泛化及安全边界。
- **JevEvolve**：[Jev 类型化决策实现无生成式维度级 ABSA](#jev-absa) 验证了冻结主干加少量校准参数的低成本结构化决策路线，而 [论点与署名：AI 评估中的来源—立场一致性偏差](#item-83) 提醒持续校准必须显式审计来源与立场交互偏差。
- **LogicEvolve**：[SPRING：SMT 求解器引导的逻辑推理过程奖励](#spring-smt) 展示了用符号求解器同时奖励有效新演绎、惩罚矛盾和空步骤的可执行方案，可直接用于构建更忠实的逻辑过程监督闭环。
- **MemoryEvolve**：[GenMem：面向自进化脚手架的生成式符号记忆](#genmem) 以稳定地址解耦记忆标识与内容演化，[MemDream：面向自探测记忆演化的离线梦境框架](#memdream) 则提供部署前主动诊断修复机制，两者可组合研究可持续且抗故障的记忆更新。
- **ResearchEvolve**：[AI Has Taste：从数学反例搜索走向自主出题](#ai-has-taste) 和 [COEVOLVE：通过证据迁移协同演化科学发现原则](#coevolve) 表明失败路线与跨分支证据都可转化为新假设，同时保持独立审查或后验隔离是防止科研闭环自我强化错误的关键。
- **SwarmEvolve**：[BaRe-Mem：面向稳健自适应智能体咨询的贝叶斯可靠性记忆](#bare-mem) 提供了按历史可靠性动态调节成员影响的机制，可与 [SAGE：自适应专家组提升多智能体推理](#sage) 的优势策略迁移和稀疏路由结合，研究可学习的群体选择与抗误导协作。

<!-- daily-summary:end -->
